that is encumbered or otherwise restricted as to its use is included in cash &#8211; restricted. As of March 31, 2026 and September 30,
2025, the balance was $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--RestrictedCashEquivalents_iI_c20260331_zjNyrbwPmwP1" title="Restricted cash"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:RestrictedCashEquivalents" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000584" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">0</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--RestrictedCashEquivalents_iI_c20250930_zKhSYdBkIxg1" title="Restricted cash"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:RestrictedCashEquivalents" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000586" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">787,365</ix:nonFraction></span>, respectively. Cash &#8211; restricted as of September 30, 2025 represents cash that was withdrawn
from the Trust Account to pay income taxes but was not utilized.</span></p>


<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: left">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000588" name="us-gaap:MarketableSecuritiesPolicy"><p id="xdx_84D_eus-gaap--MarketableSecuritiesPolicy_zbY7FCjX1mv2" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Cash
and investments held in Trust account</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As
of March 31, 2026 and September 30, 2025, all of the assets held in the Trust Account were held in money market funds which are invested
only in U.S. government securities. Investments in money market funds are presented on the balance sheet at fair value at the end of
each reporting period. Interest and dividends earned from investments in these securities are included in the statements of operations.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000590" name="IBAC:OfferingCostsPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_846_ecustom--OfferingCostsPolicyTextBlock_zPIJwO6kBpA" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Offering
Costs</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company complies with the requirements of the ASC 340-10-S99-1 and SEC Staff Accounting Bulletin (&#8220;SAB&#8221;) Topic 5A &#8212;
&#8220;Expenses of Offering&#8221;. Offering costs consist principally of professional and registration fees, cash underwriting discount,
and deferred underwriting fees incurred through the balance sheet date that are related to the Initial Public Offering. Offering costs
were allocated to the separable financial instruments issued in the Initial Public Offering based on relative fair value basis, compared
to total proceeds received. Offering costs allocated to the Public Shares were charged against the carrying value of ordinary shares
subject to possible redemption upon the completion of the Initial Public Offering and offering costs allocated to Public Rights (as defined
in Note 3) were charged to additional paid in capital at the completion of the Initial Public Offering.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000592" name="us-gaap:SharesSubjectToMandatoryRedemptionChangesInRedemptionValuePolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_843_eus-gaap--SharesSubjectToMandatoryRedemptionChangesInRedemptionValuePolicyTextBlock_zjn6iYOLpY3a" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Common
Stock Subject to Possible Redemption</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Public Shares contain a redemption feature which allows for the redemption of such Public Shares in connection with the Company&#8217;s
liquidation, or if there is a shareholder vote or tender offer in connection with the Company&#8217;s initial business combination. In
accordance with ASC 480-10-S99, the Company classifies Public Shares subject to redemption outside of permanent equity as the redemption
provisions are not solely within the control of the Company. The Public Shares sold as part of the Units in the Initial Public Offering
were issued with other freestanding instruments (i.e., Public Rights) and as such, the initial carrying value of Public Shares classified
as temporary equity are the allocated proceeds determined in accordance with ASC 470-20. The Company recognizes changes in redemption
value immediately as it occurs and will adjust the carrying value of redeemable shares to equal the redemption value at the end of each
reporting period. Immediately upon the closing of the Initial Public Offering, the Company recognized the accretion from initial book
value to redemption amount value. The change in the carrying value of redeemable shares will result in charges against retained earnings
or additional paid-in capital in the absence of retained earnings. Accordingly, as of March 31, 2026 and September 30, 2025, common stock
subject to possible redemption is presented at redemption value as temporary equity, outside of the stockholders&#8217; equity section
of the Company&#8217;s balance sheets. The Company recognizes changes in redemption value immediately as they occur and adjusts the carrying
value of redeemable shares to equal the redemption value at the end of each reporting period. Increases or decreases in the carrying
amount of redeemable shares are affected by charges against retained earnings or additional paid-in capital in the absence of retained
earnings.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
connection with the Special Meeting held on September 22, 2025, stockholders holding <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares_c20250922__20250922__us-gaap--StatementEquityComponentsAxis__us-gaap--CommonStockMember_zX1CPr63saUi" title="Redeemed shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares" contextRef="From2025-09-222025-09-22_us-gaap_CommonStockMember" id="Fact000594" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">10,009,120</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of the Company&#8217;s shares
of common stock exercised their right to redeem their shares for cash at an approximate price of $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--SharePrice_iI_c20250922__us-gaap--StatementEquityComponentsAxis__us-gaap--CommonStockMember_znTuCqFowBra" title="Share price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharePrice" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-22_us-gaap_CommonStockMember" id="Fact000596" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.60</ix:nonFraction></span> per share of the funds in the
Trust Account. As a result, approximately $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodValue_pn5n6_c20250922__20250922__us-gaap--StatementEquityComponentsAxis__us-gaap--CommonStockMember_zrxdNIgNZ2Pj" title="Redeemed value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodValue" contextRef="From2025-09-222025-09-22_us-gaap_CommonStockMember" id="Fact000598" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">106.1</ix:nonFraction></span> million was removed from the Trust Account to pay such holders, leaving approximately
$<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--RepaymentsOfDebt_pn5n6_c20250922__20250922__us-gaap--StatementEquityComponentsAxis__us-gaap--CommonStockMember_zcZqZlo6Smud" title="Repayments of debt"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:RepaymentsOfDebt" contextRef="From2025-09-222025-09-22_us-gaap_CommonStockMember" id="Fact000600" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">15.8</ix:nonFraction></span> million remaining in the Trust Account on the date of redemption.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
connection with the Special Meeting held on March 25, 2026, stockholders holding <span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares_c20260325__20260325__us-gaap--StatementEquityComponentsAxis__us-gaap--CommonStockMember_z7yJvX5V2nte" title="Redeemed shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares" contextRef="From2026-03-252026-03-25_us-gaap_CommonStockMember" id="Fact000602" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">731,741</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of the Company&#8217;s shares of common

As a result, approximately $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodValue_pn5n6_c20260325__20260325_zRqnPBA5cCp5" title="Redeemed value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodValue" contextRef="From2026-03-252026-03-25" id="Fact000606" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">7.9</ix:nonFraction></span> million was removed from the Trust Account to pay such holders, leaving approximately $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--RepaymentsOfDebt_pn5n6_c20260325__20260325_zIg7O3Qh12h2" title="Repayments of debt"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:RepaymentsOfDebt" contextRef="From2026-03-252026-03-25" id="Fact000608" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-5" scale="6" unitRef="USD">8.2</ix:nonFraction></span> million remaining
in the Trust Account on the date of redemption.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000624" name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_845_eus-gaap--IncomeTaxPolicyTextBlock_zvfggkdEVQgi" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Income
Taxes</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company accounts for income taxes under ASC 740, &#8220;Income Taxes.&#8221; ASC 740, Income Taxes, requires the recognition of deferred
tax assets and liabilities for both the expected impact of differences between the financial statements and tax basis of assets and liabilities
and for the expected future tax benefit to be derived from tax loss and tax credit carry forwards. ASC 740 additionally requires a valuation
allowance to be established when it is more likely than not that all or a portion of deferred tax assets will not be realized. As of
March 31, 2026 and September 30, 2025, the Company&#8217;s deferred tax asset had a full valuation allowance recorded against it. Our
effective tax rate was (<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations_iN_pid_dpi_uPure_c20260101__20260331_zY2EDNM3W3X1" title="Effective tax rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31" id="Fact000626" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" sign="-" unitRef="Pure">4.71</ix:nonFraction></span>)% and <span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations_pid_dp_uPure_c20250101__20250331_z54Bi6P8WfQ" title="Effective tax rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000628" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">24.16</ix:nonFraction></span>% for the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025, respectively, and (<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations_iN_pid_dpi_uPure_c20251001__20260331_zd9WSNgqzsb1" title="Effective tax rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations" contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000630" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" sign="-" unitRef="Pure">9.56</ix:nonFraction></span>)% and <span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations_pid_dp_uPure_c20241001__20250331_zP8ErvVbEHp5" title="Effective tax rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateContinuingOperations" contextRef="From2024-10-012025-03-31" id="Fact000632" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">24.13</ix:nonFraction></span>% for
the six months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025, respectively. The effective tax rate differs from the statutory tax rate of <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate_pid_dp_uPure_c20251001__20260331_z54OlynJ5Dg6" title="Statutory tax rate"><span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate_pid_dp_uPure_c20241001__20250331_zzyWtxGu0OW9" title="Statutory tax rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate" contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000634" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EffectiveIncomeTaxRateReconciliationAtFederalStatutoryIncomeTaxRate" contextRef="From2024-10-012025-03-31" id="Fact000636" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">21</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span>% for the
period ended March 31, 2026 and 2025, due to the valuation allowance on the deferred tax assets.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">ASC
740 also clarifies the accounting for uncertainty in income taxes recognized in an enterprise&#8217;s financial statements and prescribes
a recognition threshold and measurement process for financial statement recognition and measurement of a tax position taken or expected
to be taken in a tax return. For those benefits to be recognized, a tax position must be more-likely-than-not to be sustained upon examination
by taxing authorities. ASC 740 also provides guidance on derecognition, classification, interest and penalties, accounting in interim
period, disclosure and transition.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company recognizes accrued interest and penalties related to unrecognized tax benefits as income tax expense. There were <span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--UnrecognizedTaxBenefits_iI_do_c20260331_zWnJSQX3on72" title="Unrecognized tax benefits"><span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--UnrecognizedTaxBenefits_iI_do_c20250930_ztg0rCGBvVC4" title="Unrecognized tax benefits"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:UnrecognizedTaxBenefits" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000638" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:UnrecognizedTaxBenefits" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000640" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">no</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> unrecognized
tax benefits and <span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--UnrecognizedTaxBenefitsIncomeTaxPenaltiesAndInterestAccrued_iI_do_c20260331_zu0WcDyZ1MT8" title="Unrecognized tax benefits, accrued for interest and penalties"><span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--UnrecognizedTaxBenefitsIncomeTaxPenaltiesAndInterestAccrued_iI_do_c20250930_zySDpQo96ft4" title="Unrecognized tax benefits, accrued for interest and penalties"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:UnrecognizedTaxBenefitsIncomeTaxPenaltiesAndInterestAccrued" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000642" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:UnrecognizedTaxBenefitsIncomeTaxPenaltiesAndInterestAccrued" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000644" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">no</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> amounts accrued for interest and penalties as of March 31, 2026 and September 30, 2025. The Company is currently
not aware of any issues under review that could result in significant payments, accruals or material deviation from its position.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company has identified the United States as its only &#8220;major&#8221; tax jurisdiction. The Company is subject to income taxation
by major taxing authorities since inception. These examinations may include questioning the timing and amount of deductions, the nexus
of income among various tax jurisdictions and compliance with federal and state tax laws. The Company&#8217;s management does not expect
that the total amount of unrecognized tax benefits will materially change over the next twelve months.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; text-align: center">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt; text-align: center">&#160;<span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>IB
ACQUISITION CORP.</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTES
TO FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>MARCH
31, 2026</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>(Unaudited)</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center">&#160;</p>


</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000646" name="us-gaap:EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84D_eus-gaap--EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock_z6J13Q0YAIX9" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Net
(Loss) Income Per Common Stock</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company complies with the accounting and disclosure requirements of FASB ASC Topic 260, &#8220;Earnings Per Share&#8221;. Net (loss)
income per common share is computed by dividing net (loss) income by the weighted average number of shares of common stock outstanding
for the period. Remeasurement associated with the redeemable shares of common stock is excluded from earnings per share as the redemption
value approximates fair value.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
calculation of diluted net (loss) income per share does not consider the effect of the rights issued in connection with the (i) Initial
Public Offering, and (ii) the private placement since the exercise of the rights are contingent upon the occurrence of future events.
As of March 31, 2026 and September 30, 2025, the rights are exercisable to purchase <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--ClassOfWarrantOrRightNumberOfSecuritiesCalledByWarrantsOrRights_iI_c20260331_zdHWdh52l6ne" title="Warrants are exercisable to purchase, shares"><span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--ClassOfWarrantOrRightNumberOfSecuritiesCalledByWarrantsOrRights_iI_c20250930_zNhKqjLLymf8" title="Warrants are exercisable to purchase, shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightNumberOfSecuritiesCalledByWarrantsOrRights" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000648" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightNumberOfSecuritiesCalledByWarrantsOrRights" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000650" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">605,525</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> shares of common stock in the aggregate.
The weighted average of these shares was excluded from the calculation of diluted net (loss) income common stock since the inclusion
of such rights would be anti-dilutive. The rights cannot be converted to shares of common stock prior to an initial Business Combination;
therefore, they have been classified as anti-dilutive.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000652" name="us-gaap:ScheduleOfEarningsPerShareBasicAndDilutedTableTextBlock"><p id="xdx_898_eus-gaap--ScheduleOfEarningsPerShareBasicAndDilutedTableTextBlock_zj2kFVZcOcej" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
following table reflects the calculation of basic and diluted net (loss) income per common stock (in dollars, except per share amounts):</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000750" name="us-gaap:ConcentrationRiskCreditRisk"><p id="xdx_848_eus-gaap--ConcentrationRiskCreditRisk_zzRsBLQt9My" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Concentration
of Credit Risk</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Financial
instruments that potentially subject the Company to concentrations of credit risk consist of cash accounts in a financial institution,
which at times may exceed the Federal Depository Insurance Coverage of $<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--CashFDICInsuredAmount_iI_c20260331_zVfIRx0DK647" title="Federal depository insurance coverage amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashFDICInsuredAmount" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000752" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">250,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. The Company has not experienced losses on the cash account
and management believes that the Company is not exposed to significant risks on such account. Uninsured cash amounts as of March 31,
2026 and September 30, 2025, are $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--CashUninsuredAmount_iI_c20260331_z7Kh9acZsPOf" title="Cash uninsured amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashUninsuredAmount" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000754" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">0</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--CashUninsuredAmount_iI_c20250930_zsl1BAgCSKR9" title="Cash uninsured amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashUninsuredAmount" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000756" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">966,065</ix:nonFraction></span>, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000758" name="us-gaap:FairValueOfFinancialInstrumentsPolicy"><p id="xdx_84C_eus-gaap--FairValueOfFinancialInstrumentsPolicy_zE6TzQPeREZ5" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Fair
value of Financial Instruments</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
fair value of the Company&#8217;s assets and liabilities, which qualify as financial instruments under ASC Topic 820, &#8220;Fair Value
Measurements and Disclosures,&#8221; approximates the carrying amounts represented in the accompanying balance sheets, primarily due
to their short-term nature.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000762" name="IBAC:InitialPublicOfferingTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80B_ecustom--InitialPublicOfferingTextBlock_zDoQHBxTx6B7" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
3. <span id="xdx_823_zMHAVu6wAWsj">INITIAL PUBLIC OFFERING</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Pursuant
to the Initial Public Offering, the Company sold <span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction_pid_c20240328__20240328__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zFtIwcCGgdDh" title="Number of units sold"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="From2024-03-282024-03-28_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000764" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">11,500,000</ix:nonFraction></span> Units, which includes a full exercise by the underwriter of their over-allotment
option in the amount of <span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction_pid_c20240328__20240328__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_zEH6rDxFtt3h" title="Number of units sold"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="From2024-03-282024-03-28_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000766" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,500,000</ix:nonFraction></span> Units, at a purchase price of $<span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockPricePerShare_iI_pid_c20240328__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_zCFdh8C0aM9h" title="Purchase price, per unit"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2024-03-28_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000768" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per Unit. <span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockDescriptionOfTransaction_pid_c20240328__20240328__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_zN1xT9fFoLOf" title="Sale of units, description"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-03-282024-03-28_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000770" name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockDescriptionOfTransaction">Each Unit consists of one share of the Company&#8217;s
common stock, and one right. Each right entitles the holder thereof to receive one-twentieth (1/20) of one share of common stock upon
the consummation of the Business Combination.</ix:nonNumeric></span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000772" name="IBAC:PrivatePlacementDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_806_ecustom--PrivatePlacementDisclosureTextBlock_zpHBBajNsah1" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
4. <span id="xdx_82D_zmqfGVf4wJDk">PRIVATE PLACEMENT</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Simultaneously
with the closing of the Initial Public Offering, the Sponsor has purchased an aggregate of <span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction_pid_c20240328__20240328__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--PrivatePlacementMember_zrWnmnFlKMZ3" title="Number of units sold"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="From2024-03-282024-03-28_us-gaap_PrivatePlacementMember" id="Fact000774" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">610,500</ix:nonFraction></span> Private Placement Units at a price
of $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockPricePerShare_iI_pid_c20240328__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--PrivatePlacementMember_zhSszCyAX962" title="Purchase price, per unit"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2024-03-28_us-gaap_PrivatePlacementMember" id="Fact000776" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per Private Placement Unit from the Company in a private placement. Each Private Unit will consist of one share of common stock
(&#8220;Private Placement Share&#8221;) and one right (&#8220;Private Placement Right&#8221;). <span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockDescriptionOfTransaction_pid_c20240328__20240328__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--PrivatePlacementMember_zuDZzdXi2Ss5" title="Sale of units, description"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-03-282024-03-28_us-gaap_PrivatePlacementMember" id="Fact000778" name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockDescriptionOfTransaction">Each Private Placement Right will entitle
the holder to receive one-twentieth of one share of common stock at the closing of a Business Combination.</ix:nonNumeric></span> Certain proceeds from the
sale of the Private Placement Units were added to the net proceeds from the Initial Public Offering held in the Trust Account. If the
Company does not complete a Business Combination within the Combination Period, the proceeds from the sale of the Private Units will
be used to fund the redemption of the Public Shares (subject to the requirements of applicable law), and the Private Placements Units
and all underlying securities will expire worthless.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000780" name="us-gaap:RelatedPartyTransactionsDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_808_eus-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsDisclosureTextBlock_zLN7Vuyd7Cb7" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
5. <span id="xdx_829_zTL8ZTC3keOa">RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Founder
Shares</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
September 2, 2020, the Sponsor subscribed to purchase an aggregate of <span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_pid_c20200902__20200902__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_zhO8jcNjWzyc" title="Issuance of shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2020-09-022020-09-02_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000782" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">4,312,500</ix:nonFraction></span> shares (the &#8220;Founder Shares&#8221;) for a subscription
price of $<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues_pid_c20200902__20200902__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_z0lAvKOSuk55" title="Subscription price"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="From2020-09-022020-09-02_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000784" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USD">3,000</ix:nonFraction></span>. On October 26, 2023, the Sponsor agreed to surrender an aggregate of <span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares_pid_c20231026__20231026__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_zkp25TmpSrv2" title="Number of shares surrender"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockRedeemedOrCalledDuringPeriodShares" contextRef="From2023-10-262023-10-26_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000786" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,068,910</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of the Company&#8217;s common
stock for no consideration, which were cancelled, resulting in the Sponsor holding an aggregate of <span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesOutstanding_iI_pid_c20231026__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_zX1U7E1Xe2m8" title="Shares outstanding"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesOutstanding" contextRef="AsOf2023-10-26_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000788" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">3,243,590</ix:nonFraction></span> Founder Shares. The subsequent
cancellation is retrospectively reflected in the financial statements from day one.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company maintains the ownership of Founder Shares by the initial stockholders at <span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockPercentageOfOwnershipAfterTransaction_pid_dp_uPure_c20251001__20260331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_z9UTFf3iP4Jh" title="Ownership percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockPercentageOfOwnershipAfterTransaction" contextRef="From2025-10-012026-03-31_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000790" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">22.0</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the Company&#8217;s issued and outstanding
shares of common stock upon the consummation of the Initial Public Offering, not including the Private Placement Shares or the Representative
Shares. Up to <span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesOutstanding_iI_pid_c20260331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_zgbjk4FJayne" title="Common stock shares outstanding"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesOutstanding" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000792" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">423,077</ix:nonFraction></span> Founder Shares held by the initial stockholders are no longer subject to forfeiture due to the underwriters&#8217;
over-allotment option exercised in full at the Initial Public Offering.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
initial stockholders and the officers and directors have agreed not to transfer, assign or sell any of the Founder Shares until the earlier
of (i) six months after the date of the consummation of the Business Combination or (ii) the date on which the Company completes a liquidation,
merger, stock exchange or other similar transaction after its initial business combination that results in all of its public stockholders
having the right to exchange their shares of common stock for cash, securities or other property.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockDescriptionOfTransaction_pid_c20241001__20250331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_zO5ieulV8by5" title="Sale of stock description"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2024-10-012025-03-31_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000794" name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockDescriptionOfTransaction">Notwithstanding
the foregoing, if the last sale price of the Company&#8217;s common stock equals or exceeds $12.00 per share (as adjusted for stock splits,
stock dividends, reorganizations, recapitalizations and the like) for any 20 trading days within any 30-trading day period commencing
at least 150 days after its initial business combination, the Founder Shares will be released from the lock-up.</ix:nonNumeric></span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>(Unaudited)</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">From
October 2023 through January 2024, the Company&#8217;s Sponsor entered into six subscription agreements to sell membership interests
in the Sponsor to members of management, directors and director nominees. The membership interests represent the indirect equivalent
of <span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_pid_c20231001__20240131__srt--ScheduleOfEquityMethodInvestmentEquityMethodInvesteeNameAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SubscriptionAgreementMember_zGQnnud14jr" title="Number of shares issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2023-10-012024-01-31_custom_FounderSharesMember_custom_SubscriptionAgreementMember" id="Fact000804" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">525,000</ix:nonFraction></span> Founders Shares which equates to <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--EquityMethodInvestmentOwnershipPercentage_iI_dp_uPure_c20240131__srt--ScheduleOfEquityMethodInvestmentEquityMethodInvesteeNameAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--SubscriptionAgreementMember_zdrqCU0RrFW8" title="Ownership percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:EquityMethodInvestmentOwnershipPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2024-01-31_custom_FounderSharesMember_custom_SubscriptionAgreementMember" id="Fact000806" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">16.19</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the <span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesOutstanding_iI_pid_c20231026__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--FounderSharesMember_zlIUe11wPfWg" title="Number of shares held"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesOutstanding" contextRef="AsOf2023-10-26_custom_FounderSharesMember" id="Fact000808" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">3,243,590</ix:nonFraction></span> Founders Shares issued and outstanding. Founder Shares are &#8220;earned upon the completion of a successful Business Combination&#8221; and the modified agreement is to be
effective contemporaneously with the date and time of the initial subscription agreements. The sale of the membership interests to the
Company&#8217;s management, directors and director&#8217;s nominees is in the scope of FASB ASC Topic 718, &#8220;Compensation-Stock
Compensation&#8221; (&#8220;ASC 718&#8221;). Under ASC 718, stock-based compensation associated with equity-classified awards is measured
at fair value upon the grant date.</span></p>

The Founders Shares were granted subject
to a performance condition (i.e., the occurrence of a Business Combination). Compensation expense related to the Founders Shares is recognized
only when the performance condition is probable of occurrence under the applicable accounting literature in this circumstance. As of
March 31, 2026, the Company determined that a Business Combination is not considered probable, and, therefore, no stock-based compensation
expense has been recognized. Stock-based compensation would be recognized at the date a Business Combination is considered probable (i.e.,
upon consummation of a Business Combination) in an amount equal to the number of Founders Shares times the grant date fair value per
share (unless subsequently modified) less the amount initially received for the purchase of the Founders Shares.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_8A4_zJuaYxkCGvQ5" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Administrative
Support Agreement</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company entered into an Administrative Services Agreement pursuant to which the Company agreed to pay the Chief Financial Officer a sum
of $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--OfficersCompensation_c20231001__20231001__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_zyXMx1vEaVzk" title="Monthly payment officer"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OfficersCompensation" contextRef="From2023-10-012023-10-01_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember" id="Fact000865" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">5,000</ix:nonFraction></span> per month commencing on October 1, 2023. Upon completion of the initial business combination or the liquidation, the Company
will cease paying these monthly fees. On January 22, 2024, the Company&#8217;s Chief Financial Officer resigned, and the Administrative
Services Agreement was terminated.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
January 22, 2024, the Company appointed a new Chief Financial Officer and entered into an Administrative Services Agreement dated January
24, 2024, pursuant to which the Company agreed to pay the Chief Financial Officer a sum of $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--OfficersCompensation_c20240122__20240122__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_zIjBHQyOO3fe" title="Monthly payment officer"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OfficersCompensation" contextRef="From2024-01-222024-01-22_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember" id="Fact000867" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">5,000</ix:nonFraction></span> per month commencing at the time of
the Initial Public Offering closing. The agreement further specified that upon completion of the initial business combination or the
liquidation, the Company will cease paying these monthly fees.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">For
the three and six months ended March 31, 2026, the Company incurred $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--ProfessionalFees_c20260101__20260331__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_zRMNUejrBxW8" title="Services fees"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProfessionalFees" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember" id="Fact000869" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">15,000</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--ProfessionalFees_c20251001__20260331__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_z84Ba8EWm74k" title="Services fees"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProfessionalFees" contextRef="From2025-10-012026-03-31_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember" id="Fact000871" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">30,000</ix:nonFraction></span> in fees for these services, respectively, of
which $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--AccruedLiabilitiesCurrent_iI_c20260331__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_ztVN3ukxOJ4k" title="Accrued expenses"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AccruedLiabilitiesCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember" id="Fact000873" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">15,000</ix:nonFraction></span> is recorded as accrued expenses in the balance sheet as of March 31, 2026. For the three and six months ended March 31,
2025, the Company incurred $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--ProfessionalFees_c20250101__20250331__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_ztcg5o6aAAL2" title="Services fees"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProfessionalFees" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember" id="Fact000875" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">15,000</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--ProfessionalFees_c20241001__20250331__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_zVC8Qjx7XUw8" title="Services fees"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProfessionalFees" contextRef="From2024-10-012025-03-31_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember" id="Fact000877" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">30,000</ix:nonFraction></span> in fees for these services, respectively, of which $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--AccruedLiabilitiesCurrent_iI_c20250331__srt--TitleOfIndividualAxis__srt--ChiefFinancialOfficerMember_zgnwKoCuutah" title="Accrued expenses"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AccruedLiabilitiesCurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-03-31_srt_ChiefFinancialOfficerMember" id="Fact000879" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">15,000</ix:nonFraction></span> is recorded as accounts payable
and accrued expenses in the balance sheet as of March 31, 2025.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000881" name="us-gaap:CommitmentsAndContingenciesDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_806_eus-gaap--CommitmentsAndContingenciesDisclosureTextBlock_zYee9vuSDiO3" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
6. <span id="xdx_823_zwvkmRCtSuvj">COMMITMENTS AND CONTINGENCY</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Registration
Rights</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
holders of the Founder Shares, Private Placement Units (and their underlying securities) any Units that may be issued upon conversion
of the Working Capital Loans (and underlying securities), and Representative Shares are entitled to registration rights pursuant to a
registration rights agreement signed on the effective date of the Initial Public Offering requiring the Company to register such securities
for resale. The holders of these securities are entitled to make up to three demands, excluding short form demands, that the Company
register such securities. In addition, the holders have certain &#8220;piggy-back&#8221; registration rights with respect to registration
statements filed subsequent to the completion of a Business Combination and rights to require the Company to register for resale such
securities pursuant to Rule 415 under the Securities Act. The registration rights agreement does not contain liquidated damages or other
cash settlement provisions resulting from delays in registering the Company&#8217;s securities. The Company will bear the expenses incurred
in connection with the filing of any such registration statements.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>Underwriting
Agreement and Business Combination Marketing Agreement</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company engaged I-Bankers to act as Underwriters on the Initial Public Offering of the Company&#8217;s Units, for $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues_c20251001__20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--UnderwritersAgreementMember__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zj8nletpIlpf" title="Stock issued during period value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="From2025-10-012026-03-31_custom_UnderwritersAgreementMember_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000883" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">115,000,000</ix:nonFraction></span> and the
simultaneous listing on the Nasdaq Global Market. The Underwriters had a 30-day option to purchase up to an additional <span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues_c20251001__20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--UnderwritersAgreementMember__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zDKjIORglxZb" title="Stock issued during period shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="From2025-10-012026-03-31_custom_UnderwritersAgreementMember_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000885" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,500,000</ix:nonFraction></span> Units
to cover over-allotments at the Initial Public Offering price, less the underwriting discounts and commissions. On March 28, 2024, simultaneously
with the closing of the Initial Public Offering, the Underwriters elected to fully exercise the over-allotment option to purchase an
additional <span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction_pid_c20240328__20240328__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_zs5fCEw8PyMk" title="Number of units sold"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="From2024-03-282024-03-28_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000887" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,500,000</ix:nonFraction></span> Units at a price of $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockPricePerShare_iI_pid_c20240328__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_zGQJ32FIK7Yl" title="Purchase price, per unit"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockPricePerShare" contextRef="AsOf2024-03-28_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000889" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per Unit.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Underwriters were entitled to a cash underwriting discount of $<span id="xdx_901_ecustom--CashUnderwritingDiscountPerUnit_iI_pid_c20260331__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--UnderwritersAgreementMember_z08vGfgjswb1" title="Cash underwriting discount per unit"><ix:nonFraction name="IBAC:CashUnderwritingDiscountPerUnit" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_us-gaap_IPOMember_custom_UnderwritersAgreementMember" id="Fact000891" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">0.30</ix:nonFraction></span> per Unit, or $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--PaymentsForUnderwritingExpense_c20251001__20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--UnderwritersAgreementMember__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zyMX8bwfSjs" title="Cash underwriting fees"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsForUnderwritingExpense" contextRef="From2025-10-012026-03-31_custom_UnderwritersAgreementMember_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000893" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">3,450,000</ix:nonFraction></span> in the aggregate, paid upon the closing of
the Initial Public Offering. In addition, under a business combination marketing agreement, the Company engaged I-Bankers to provide
marketing services in connection with the Business Combination and will pay I-Bankers a cash fee for such marketing services upon the
consummation of the Business Combination in an amount equal to, in the aggregate, <span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--ManagementAndServiceFeesRate_pid_dp_uPure_c20251001__20260331__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--UnderwritersAgreementMember_zamqKZR548Ja" title="Percentage of cash fee for service"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ManagementAndServiceFeesRate" contextRef="From2025-10-012026-03-31_custom_UnderwritersAgreementMember_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000895" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">3.5</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the gross proceeds of the Initial Public Offering
(the &#8220;M&amp;A fee&#8221;) or $<span id="xdx_90D_ecustom--MergersAndAcquisitionsCashFee_c20251001__20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--UnderwritersAgreementMember__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zDbIc3fRnpYe" title="M&amp;A cash fee"><ix:nonFraction name="IBAC:MergersAndAcquisitionsCashFee" contextRef="From2025-10-012026-03-31_custom_UnderwritersAgreementMember_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000897" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">4,025,000</ix:nonFraction></span> in the aggregate. If the Company doesn&#8217;t complete a business combination, no fee
will be due. In addition, the Company will pay the I-Bankers a finder fee equal to <span id="xdx_90B_ecustom--PercentageOfFinderFee_pid_dp_uPure_c20251001__20260331__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--UnderwritersAgreementMember_zTMMwJZ7fQNi" title="Percentage of finder fee"><ix:nonFraction name="IBAC:PercentageOfFinderFee" contextRef="From2025-10-012026-03-31_custom_UnderwritersAgreementMember_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000899" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">1.0</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the consideration issued to a target if the
business combination is consummated with a target introduced by the I-Bankers.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
the closing of the Initial Public Offering, pursuant to the underwriting agreement, the Company issued as compensation <span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesShareBasedCompensationGross_c20251001__20260331__us-gaap--TypeOfArrangementAxis__custom--UnderwritersAgreementMember__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_z2Og7Tg1N5re" title="Shares issued for compensation"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesShareBasedCompensationGross" contextRef="From2025-10-012026-03-31_custom_UnderwritersAgreementMember_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000901" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">395,000</ix:nonFraction></span> shares
of common stock for no cash consideration (the &#8220;Representative Shares&#8221;).</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Working

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">On
September 16, 2024, the Company issued an unsecured promissory note in the principal amount of $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentFaceAmount_iI_c20240916__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__custom--UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--SuDeTangGlobalCorporationMember_zJkslOunaeBb" title="Principal amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentFaceAmount" contextRef="AsOf2024-09-16_custom_UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember_custom_SuDeTangGlobalCorporationMember" id="Fact000903" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">150,000</ix:nonFraction></span> to Su De Tang Global Corporation
(the &#8220;Working Capital Loan&#8221;). The principal balance of this Promissory Note represents the first of potentially three instalments
of the Working Capital Loan. The Working Capital Loan bears no interest and will be extinguished without any payment required at the
consummation of a Business Combination with Su De Tang Global Corporation. The Company has borrowed a total of $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--OtherBorrowings_iI_c20260331__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__custom--UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--SuDeTangGlobalCorporationMember_zSZVcFSUggGi" title="Borrowings"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherBorrowings" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember_custom_SuDeTangGlobalCorporationMember" id="Fact000905" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">147,629</ix:nonFraction></span> under the Working
Capital Loan. As of September 30, 2025, the Company repaid an amount of $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--RepaymentsOfDebt_c20241001__20250930__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__custom--UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember__dei--LegalEntityAxis__custom--SuDeTangGlobalCorporationMember_zsUDaZzrtTZ3" title="Repayment of loan"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:RepaymentsOfDebt" contextRef="From2024-10-012025-09-30_custom_UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember_custom_SuDeTangGlobalCorporationMember" id="Fact000907" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">147,629</ix:nonFraction></span>. Borrowings under the note are no longer available.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Common

of common stock are entitled to one vote for each share.</ix:nonNumeric></span> As of March 31, 2026 and September 30, 2025, there were <span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesIssued_iI_c20260331_zSXVUAXeKq18" title="Common stock, shares issued"><span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesOutstanding_iI_c20260331_zv4ARA8007T5" title="Common stock, shares outstanding"><span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesIssued_iI_c20250930_zZf49337qjZ9" title="Common stock, shares issued"><span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--CommonStockSharesOutstanding_iI_c20250930_zdUUUdR2iVEj" title="Common stock, shares outstanding"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesIssued" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000929" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesOutstanding" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000931" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesIssued" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000933" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockSharesOutstanding" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000935" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">4,249,090</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span></span></span> shares of
common stock issued and outstanding, excluding <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--SharesSubjectToMandatoryRedemptionSettlementTermsNumberOfShares_iI_c20260331_z3ZDw8jaiote" title="Common stock subject to possible redemption"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesSubjectToMandatoryRedemptionSettlementTermsNumberOfShares" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000937" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">759,139</ix:nonFraction></span> and <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--SharesSubjectToMandatoryRedemptionSettlementTermsNumberOfShares_iI_c20250930_zZbyRfp8SvLl" title="Common stock subject to possible redemption"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesSubjectToMandatoryRedemptionSettlementTermsNumberOfShares" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000939" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">1,490,880</ix:nonFraction></span> shares of common stock subject to possible redemption, respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>IB
ACQUISITION CORP.</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTES
TO FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>MARCH
31, 2026</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>(Unaudited)</b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000941" name="us-gaap:FairValueMeasurementInputsDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_801_eus-gaap--FairValueMeasurementInputsDisclosureTextBlock_zP6GK2r1wrXl" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
8. <span id="xdx_823_zfZcel0WSung">FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company follows the guidance in ASC 820 for its financial assets and liabilities that are re-measured and reported at fair value at each
reporting period, and non-financial assets and liabilities that are measured and reported at fair value at least annually.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
fair value of the Company&#8217;s financial assets and liabilities reflects management&#8217;s estimate of amounts that the Company would
have received in connection with the sale of the assets or paid in connection with the transfer of the liabilities in an orderly transaction
between market participants at the measurement date. In connection with measuring the fair value of its assets and liabilities, the Company
seeks to maximize the use of observable inputs (market data obtained from independent sources) and to minimize the use of unobservable
inputs (internal assumptions about how market participants would price assets and liabilities). The following fair value hierarchy is
used to classify assets and liabilities based on the observable inputs and unobservable inputs used in order to value the assets and
liabilities:</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 14.55pt"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse">
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 0.5in; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 0.75in; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Level 1:</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Quoted prices
    in active markets for identical assets or liabilities. An active market for an asset or liability is a market in which transactions
    for the asset or liability occur with sufficient frequency and volume to provide pricing information on an ongoing basis.</span></td></tr>
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td></tr>
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Level 2:</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Observable inputs other
    than Level 1 inputs. Examples of Level 2 inputs include quoted prices in active markets for similar assets or liabilities and quoted
    prices for identical assets or liabilities in markets that are not active.</span></td></tr>
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td></tr>
  <tr style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; vertical-align: top">
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Level 3:</span></td>
    <td style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Unobservable inputs based
    on our assessment of the assumptions that market participants would use in pricing the asset or liability.</span></td></tr>
  </table>
<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As
of March 31, 2026, assets held in the Trust Account were comprised of $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent_iI_c20260331_z4DXdKAqQnVk" title="Assets held in the trust account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000943" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">8,188,994</ix:nonFraction></span> in a money market fund that is invested primarily in
U.S. Treasury Securities. For the quarterly period ended March 31, 2026, the Company had withdrawn $<span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--InterestExpenseTrustPreferredSecurities_c20260101__20260331_zXbTaJjjByje" title="Interest earned on trust account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InterestExpenseTrustPreferredSecurities" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31" id="Fact000945" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">105,000</ix:nonFraction></span> of interest earned on the
Trust Account to pay for income taxes and $<span id="xdx_908_ecustom--RedemptionsOfCommonStock_iN_di_c20251001__20260331_zmjepOrfTTGg" title="Redemptions trust Account"><ix:nonFraction name="IBAC:RedemptionsOfCommonStock" contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000947" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" sign="-" unitRef="USD">7,886,591</ix:nonFraction></span> from the Trust Account in connection with redemption.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">As
of September 30, 2025, assets held in the Trust Account were comprised of $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent_iI_c20250930_zLqvM56TCRW8" title="Assets held in the trust account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrustNoncurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-09-30" id="Fact000949" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">15,890,194</ix:nonFraction></span> in a money market fund that is invested primarily
in U.S. Treasury Securities. For the year ended September 30, 2025, the Company had withdrawn $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--InterestExpenseTrustPreferredSecurities_c20241001__20250930_zHtvGAbO48i5" title="Interest earned on trust account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InterestExpenseTrustPreferredSecurities" contextRef="From2024-10-012025-09-30" id="Fact000951" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,711,366</ix:nonFraction></span> of interest earned on the Trust
Account to pay for income taxes and $<span id="xdx_90A_ecustom--RedemptionsOfCommonStock_iN_di_c20241001__20250930_zhBL8DHl2NIi" title="Redemptions trust Account"><ix:nonFraction name="IBAC:RedemptionsOfCommonStock" contextRef="From2024-10-012025-09-30" id="Fact000953" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" sign="-" unitRef="USD">106,131,025</ix:nonFraction></span> from the Trust Account in connection with redemptions.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-10-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000955" name="us-gaap:FairValueAssetsMeasuredOnRecurringBasisTextBlock"><p id="xdx_89C_eus-gaap--FairValueAssetsMeasuredOnRecurringBasisTextBlock_zn8e8TtK8Et7" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
